.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:40:// The body's checks, in the order the first failing one decides the 400 message.
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:41:function parseBody(body) {
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:42:  const unknown = Object.keys(body).find(key => !BODY_FIELDS.includes(key))
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:43:  if (unknown !== undefined) throw new ValidationError(`unknown field ${unknown}`)

.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:52:function parseEntry(entry, group) {

.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:62:        payer: check('bad-payer', () => parseMember(entry.payer, group.members, PAYER_MESSAGE)),
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:63:        amount: check('bad-amount', () => parseAmount(entry.amount)),
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:64:        split: check('bad-split', () => parseSplit(entry.split, group.members)),

.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:66:    }
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:67:  }

.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:78:// Sorts the entries into { problems, skipped, accepted }: one problem per entry that breaks a rule, the indexes

.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:80:function sortEntries(state, group, entries, skipRecorded) {
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:81:  const recorded = new Set(state.expenses.filter(e => e.group === group.id).map(expenseKey))
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:82:  // Every expense that passed its field checks, so a later copy is a duplicate even of a skipped one.
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:83:  const seen = new Set()
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:84:  const problems = []
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:85:  const skipped = []
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:86:  const accepted = []
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:87:  for (const [index, entry] of entries.entries()) {
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:88:    let parsed
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:89:    try {
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:90:      parsed = parseEntry(entry, group)
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:91:    } catch (err) {
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:92:      if (!(err instanceof RuleError)) throw err
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:93:      problems.push({ index, rule: err.message })
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:94:      continue
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:95:    }
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:96:    if (parsed.type === 'expense') {
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:97:      const key = expenseKey(parsed.fields)
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:98:      const isDuplicate = seen.has(key)
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:99:      seen.add(key)
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:100:      if (isDuplicate) {
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:101:        problems.push({ index, rule: 'duplicate' })
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:102:        continue
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:103:      }
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:104:      if (recorded.has(key)) {
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:105:        if (skipRecorded) skipped.push(index)
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:106:        else problems.push({ index, rule: 'already-recorded' })
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:107:        continue
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:108:      }
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:109:    }
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:110:    accepted.push(parsed)
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:111:  }
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:112:  return { problems, skipped, accepted }
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:113:}

.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:115:// The store with the accepted entries added, each kind numbered on from its next id in entry order.
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:116:function addEntries(state, group, accepted) {
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:117:  let { nextExpenseId, nextPaymentId } = state
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:118:  const expenses = []
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:119:  const payments = []
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:120:  for (const { type, fields } of accepted) {
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:121:    if (type === 'expense') expenses.push({ id: nextExpenseId++, group: group.id, ...fields })
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:122:    else payments.push({ id: nextPaymentId++, group: group.id, ...fields })
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:123:  }
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:124:  const next = {
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:125:    ...state,
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:126:    expenses: [...state.expenses, ...expenses],
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:127:    payments: [...state.payments, ...payments],
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:128:    nextExpenseId,
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:129:    nextPaymentId,
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:130:  }
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:131:  return { expenses, payments, next }
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:132:}

.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:134:export function importEntries({ state, params, body }) {
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:135:  const group = findGroup(state, params[0])
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:136:  if (!group) return NOT_FOUND
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:137:  const { entries, dryRun, skipRecorded } = parseBody(body)
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:138:  const { problems, skipped, accepted } = sortEntries(state, group, entries, skipRecorded)
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:139:  if (problems.length > 0) return { status: 422, body: { error: 'import rejected', problems } }
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:140:
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:141:  const { expenses, payments, next } = addEntries(state, group, accepted)
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:142:  const reply = {
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:143:    expenses: expenses.map(expenseView),
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:144:    payments: payments.map(paymentView),
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:145:    skipped,
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:146:    balances: balances(next, group),
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:147:  }
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:148:  if (dryRun) return { status: 200, body: reply }
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:149:  // Nothing to record (every entry skipped): reply as a success but leave the data file alone.
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:150:  return { status: 201, body: reply, ...(accepted.length > 0 && { state: next }) }
.team/near-miss/26-import.mjs:151:}

.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:78:// Sorts the entries into { problems, skipped, accepted }: one problem per entry that breaks a rule, the indexes

.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:80:function sortEntries(state, group, entries, skipRecorded) {
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:81:  const recorded = new Set(state.expenses.filter(e => e.group === group.id).map(expenseKey))
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:82:  // Every expense that passed its field checks, so a later copy is a duplicate even of a skipped one.
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:83:  const seen = new Set()
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:84:  const problems = []
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:85:  const skipped = []
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:86:  const accepted = []
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:87:  for (const [index, entry] of entries.entries()) {
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:88:    let parsed
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:89:    try {
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:90:      parsed = parseEntry(entry, group)
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:91:    } catch (err) {
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:92:      if (!(err instanceof RuleError)) throw err
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:93:      problems.push({ index, rule: err.message })
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:94:      continue
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:95:    }
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:96:    if (parsed.type === 'expense') {
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:97:      const key = expenseKey(parsed.fields)
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:98:      const isDuplicate = seen.has(key)
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:99:      seen.add(key)
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:100:      if (isDuplicate) {
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:101:        problems.push({ index, rule: 'duplicate' })
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:102:        continue
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:103:      }
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:104:      if (recorded.has(key)) {
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:105:        if (skipRecorded) skipped.push(index)
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:106:        else problems.push({ index, rule: 'already-recorded' })
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:107:        continue
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:108:      }
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:109:    }
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:110:    accepted.push(parsed)
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:111:  }
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:112:  return { problems, skipped, accepted }
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:113:}
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:114:
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:115:// The store with the accepted entries added, each kind numbered on from its next id in entry order.
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:116:function addEntries(state, group, accepted) {
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:117:  let { nextExpenseId, nextPaymentId } = state
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:118:  const expenses = []
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:119:  const payments = []
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:120:  for (const { type, fields } of accepted) {
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:121:    if (type === 'expense') expenses.push({ id: nextExpenseId++, group: group.id, ...fields })
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:122:    else payments.push({ id: nextPaymentId++, group: group.id, ...fields })
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:123:  }
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:124:  const next = {
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:125:    ...state,
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:126:    expenses: [...state.expenses, ...expenses],
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:127:    payments: [...state.payments, ...payments],
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:128:    nextExpenseId,
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:129:    nextPaymentId,
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:130:  }
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:131:  return { expenses, payments, next }
.team/near-miss/16-import.mjs:132:}

.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:52:function parseEntry(entry, group) {

.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:79:// of the already-recorded entries skip_recorded leaves out, and the parsed entries to import, all in entry order.
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:80:function sortEntries(state, group, entries, skipRecorded) {
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:81:  const recorded = new Set(state.expenses.filter(e => e.group === group.id).map(expenseKey))
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:82:  // Every expense that passed its field checks, so a later copy is a duplicate even of a skipped one.
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:83:  const seen = new Set()
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:84:  const problems = []
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:85:  const skipped = []
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:86:  const accepted = []
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:87:  for (const [index, entry] of entries.entries()) {
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:88:    let parsed
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:89:    try {
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:90:      parsed = parseEntry(entry, group)
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:91:    } catch (err) {
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:92:      if (!(err instanceof RuleError)) throw err
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:93:      problems.push({ index, rule: err.message })
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:94:      continue
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:95:    }
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:96:    if (parsed.type === 'expense') {
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:97:      const key = expenseKey(parsed.fields)
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:98:      const isDuplicate = seen.has(key)
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:99:      seen.add(key)
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:100:      if (isDuplicate) {
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:101:        problems.push({ index, rule: 'duplicate' })
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:102:        continue
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:103:      }
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:104:      if (recorded.has(key)) {
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:105:        if (skipRecorded) skipped.push(index)
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:106:        else problems.push({ index, rule: 'already-recorded' })
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:107:        continue
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:108:      }
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:109:    }
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:110:    accepted.push(parsed)
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:111:  }
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:112:  return { problems, skipped, accepted }
.team/near-miss/13-import.mjs:113:}

.team/near-miss/06-import.mjs:82:  // Every expense that passed its field checks, so a later copy is a duplicate even of a skipped one.

.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:78:// Sorts the entries into { problems, skipped, accepted }: one problem per entry that breaks a rule, the indexes
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:79:// of the already-recorded entries skip_recorded leaves out, and the parsed entries to import, all in entry order.
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:80:function sortEntries(state, group, entries, skipRecorded) {
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:81:  const recorded = new Set(state.expenses.filter(e => e.group === group.id).map(expenseKey))
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:82:  // Every expense that passed its field checks, so a later copy is a duplicate even of a skipped one.
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:83:  const seen = new Set()
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:84:  const problems = []
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:85:  const skipped = []
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:86:  const accepted = []
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:87:  for (const [index, entry] of entries.entries()) {
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:88:    let parsed
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:89:    try {
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:90:      parsed = parseEntry(entry, group)
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:91:    } catch (err) {
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:92:      if (!(err instanceof RuleError)) throw err
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:93:      problems.push({ index, rule: err.message })
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:94:      continue
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:95:    }
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:96:    if (parsed.type === 'expense') {
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:97:      const key = expenseKey(parsed.fields)
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:98:      const isDuplicate = seen.has(key)
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:99:      seen.add(key)
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:100:      if (isDuplicate) {
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:101:        problems.push({ index, rule: 'duplicate' })
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:102:        continue
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:103:      }
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:104:      if (recorded.has(key)) {
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:105:        if (skipRecorded) skipped.push(index)
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:106:        else problems.push({ index, rule: 'already-recorded' })
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:107:        continue
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:108:      }
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:109:    }
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:110:    accepted.push(parsed)
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:111:  }
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:112:  return { problems, skipped, accepted }
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:113:}
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:114:
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:115:// The store with the accepted entries added, each kind numbered on from its next id in entry order.
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:116:function addEntries(state, group, accepted) {
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:117:  let { nextExpenseId, nextPaymentId } = state
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:118:  const expenses = []
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:119:  const payments = []
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:120:  for (const { type, fields } of accepted) {
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:121:    if (type === 'expense') expenses.push({ id: nextExpenseId++, group: group.id, ...fields })
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:122:    else payments.push({ id: nextPaymentId++, group: group.id, ...fields })
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:123:  }
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:124:  const next = {
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:125:    ...state,
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:126:    expenses: [...state.expenses, ...expenses],
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:127:    payments: [...state.payments, ...payments],
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:128:    nextExpenseId,
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:129:    nextPaymentId,
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:130:  }
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:131:  return { expenses, payments, next }
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:132:}

.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:134:export function importEntries({ state, params, body }) {

.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:136:  if (!group) return NOT_FOUND
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:137:  const { entries, dryRun, skipRecorded } = parseBody(body)

.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:139:  if (problems.length > 0) return { status: 422, body: { error: 'import rejected', problems } }
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:140:
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:141:  const { expenses, payments, next } = addEntries(state, group, accepted)
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:142:  const reply = {
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:143:    expenses: expenses.map(expenseView),
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:144:    payments: payments.map(paymentView),
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:145:    skipped,
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:146:    balances: balances(next, group),
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:147:  }
.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:148:  if (dryRun) return { status: 200, body: reply }

.team/near-miss/01-import.mjs:150:  return { status: 201, body: reply, ...(accepted.length > 0 && { state: next }) }

.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:51:// The clean { type, fields } of one entry, or a RuleError naming the first rule it breaks before `duplicate`.
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:52:function parseEntry(entry, group) {
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:53:  if (!isObject(entry)) throw new RuleError('not-an-object')
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:54:  const { type } = entry
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:55:  if (type !== 'expense' && type !== 'payment') throw new RuleError('bad-type')

.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:64:    if (hasUnknown) throw new RuleError('unknown-field')

.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:75:// Two expenses are the same when payer, amount in cents and trimmed, lower-cased description match.
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:76:const expenseKey = ({ payer, amount, description }) =>
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:77:  JSON.stringify([payer, amount, description.trim().toLowerCase()])
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:78:
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:79:// Sorts the entries into { problems, skipped, accepted }: one problem per entry that breaks a rule, the indexes
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:80:// of the already-recorded entries skip_recorded leaves out, and the parsed entries to import, all in entry order.
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:81:function sortEntries(state, group, entries, skipRecorded) {
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:82:  const recorded = new Set(state.expenses.filter(e => e.group === group.id).map(expenseKey))
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:83:  // Every expense that passed its field checks, so a later copy is a duplicate even of a skipped one.
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:84:  const seen = new Set()
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:85:  const problems = []
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:86:  const skipped = []
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:87:  const accepted = []
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:88:  for (const [index, entry] of entries.entries()) {
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:89:    let parsed
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:90:    try {
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:91:      parsed = parseEntry(entry, group)
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:92:    } catch (err) {
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:93:      if (!(err instanceof RuleError)) throw err
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:94:      problems.push({ index, rule: err.message })
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:95:      continue
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:96:    }
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:97:    if (parsed.type === 'expense') {
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:98:      const key = expenseKey(parsed.fields)
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:99:      const isDuplicate = seen.has(key)
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:100:      seen.add(key)
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:101:      if (isDuplicate) {
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:102:        problems.push({ index, rule: 'duplicate' })
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:103:        continue
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:104:      }
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:105:      if (recorded.has(key)) {
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:106:        if (skipRecorded) skipped.push(index)
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:107:        else problems.push({ index, rule: 'already-recorded' })
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:108:        continue
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:109:      }
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:110:    }
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:111:    accepted.push(parsed)
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:112:  }
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:113:  return { problems, skipped, accepted }
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:114:}
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:115:
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:116:// The store with the accepted entries added, each kind numbered on from its next id in entry order.
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:117:function addEntries(state, group, accepted) {
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:118:  let { nextExpenseId, nextPaymentId } = state
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:119:  const expenses = []
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:120:  const payments = []
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:121:  for (const { type, fields } of accepted) {
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:122:    if (type === 'expense') expenses.push({ id: nextExpenseId++, group: group.id, ...fields })
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:123:    else payments.push({ id: nextPaymentId++, group: group.id, ...fields })
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:124:  }
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:125:  const next = {
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:126:    ...state,
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:127:    expenses: [...state.expenses, ...expenses],
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:128:    payments: [...state.payments, ...payments],
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:129:    nextExpenseId,
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:130:    nextPaymentId,
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:131:  }
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:132:  return { expenses, payments, next }
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:133:}
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:134:
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:135:export function importEntries({ state, params, body }) {
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:136:  const group = findGroup(state, params[0])
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:137:  if (!group) return NOT_FOUND
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:138:  const { entries, dryRun, skipRecorded } = parseBody(body)
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:139:  const { problems, skipped, accepted } = sortEntries(state, group, entries, skipRecorded)

.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:141:
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:142:  const { expenses, payments, next } = addEntries(state, group, accepted)
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:143:  const reply = {
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:144:    expenses: expenses.map(expenseView),
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:145:    payments: payments.map(paymentView),
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:146:    skipped,
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:147:    balances: balances(next, group),
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:148:  }
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:149:  if (dryRun) return { status: 200, body: reply }
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:150:  // Nothing to record (every entry skipped): reply as a success but leave the data file alone.
.team/near-miss/23-import.mjs:151:  return { status: 201, body: reply, ...(accepted.length > 0 && { state: next }) }

.team/near-miss/28-validate.mjs:20:// Digits with at most two decimals, as String() writes a number: "12", "12.5", "4.35".
.team/near-miss/28-validate.mjs:21:const TWO_DECIMALS = /^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]{1,2})?$/
.team/near-miss/28-validate.mjs:22:

.team/near-miss/28-validate.mjs:25:

.team/near-miss/28-validate.mjs:27:
.team/near-miss/28-validate.mjs:28:export function parseGroupId(v) {
.team/near-miss/28-validate.mjs:29:  if (typeof v !== 'string' || !GROUP_ID.test(v)) {
.team/near-miss/28-validate.mjs:30:    throw new ValidationError('id must be 1 to 30 characters of a-z, 0-9 or -, starting with a letter')
.team/near-miss/28-validate.mjs:31:  }
.team/near-miss/28-validate.mjs:32:  return v

.team/near-miss/28-validate.mjs:38:  }
.team/near-miss/28-validate.mjs:39:  return v

.team/near-miss/28-validate.mjs:41:

.team/near-miss/28-validate.mjs:46:  }
.team/near-miss/28-validate.mjs:47:  if (!v.every(name => typeof name === 'string' && MEMBER_NAME.test(name))) {
.team/near-miss/28-validate.mjs:48:    throw new ValidationError('member names must be 1 to 30 characters of A-Z, a-z, 0-9, _ or -, starting with a letter')

.team/near-miss/28-validate.mjs:51:  return [...v]

.team/near-miss/28-validate.mjs:60:
.team/near-miss/28-validate.mjs:61:// Amounts arrive as numbers such as 12.5 or 4.35 and are kept as whole cents, so sums never drift.
.team/near-miss/28-validate.mjs:62:export function parseAmount(v) {

.team/near-miss/28-validate.mjs:64:  // Rounded, not truncated: 4.35 * 100 is 434.99999999999994 in floating point.

.team/near-miss/08-import.mjs:82:  // Every expense that passed its field checks, so a later copy is a duplicate even of a skipped one.

.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:80:function sortEntries(state, group, entries, skipRecorded) {
.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:81:  const recorded = new Set(state.expenses.filter(e => e.group === group.id).map(expenseKey))
.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:82:  // Every expense that passed its field checks, so a later copy is a duplicate even of a skipped one.
.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:83:  const seen = new Set()

.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:90:      parsed = parseEntry(entry, group)

.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:92:      if (!(err instanceof RuleError)) throw err
.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:93:      problems.push({ index, rule: err.message })
.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:94:      continue
.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:95:    }
.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:96:    if (parsed.type === 'expense') {
.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:97:      const key = expenseKey(parsed.fields)
.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:98:      const isDuplicate = seen.has(key)
.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:99:      seen.add(key)
.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:100:      if (isDuplicate) {
.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:101:        problems.push({ index, rule: 'duplicate' })
.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:102:        continue
.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:103:      }
.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:104:      if (recorded.has(key)) {
.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:105:        if (skipRecorded) skipped.push(index)
.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:106:        else problems.push({ index, rule: 'already-recorded' })
.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:107:        continue
.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:108:      }

.team/near-miss/20-import.mjs:116:function addEntries(state, group, accepted) {

.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:21:const isObject = v => v !== null && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v)

.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:23:// Runs parse and returns its value; a ValidationError becomes the given rule.

.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:26:    return parse()

.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:33:// A flag that is false when its key is absent, and must be a boolean when present.

.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:51:// The clean { type, fields } of one entry, or a RuleError naming the first rule it breaks before `duplicate`.
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:52:function parseEntry(entry, group) {
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:53:  if (!isObject(entry)) throw new RuleError('not-an-object')

.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:55:  if (type !== 'expense' && type !== 'payment') throw new RuleError('bad-type')

.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:57:  if (type === 'expense') {
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:58:    return {
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:59:      type,
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:60:      fields: {
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:61:        description: check('bad-description', () => parseDescription(entry.description)),
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:62:        payer: check('bad-payer', () => parseMember(entry.payer, group.members, PAYER_MESSAGE)),
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:63:        amount: check('bad-amount', () => parseAmount(entry.amount)),
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:64:        split: check('bad-split', () => parseSplit(entry.split, group.members)),
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:65:      },
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:66:    }
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:67:  }
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:68:  const from = check('bad-from', () => parseMember(entry.from, group.members, MEMBER_MESSAGE))
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:69:  const to = check('bad-to', () => parseMember(entry.to, group.members, MEMBER_MESSAGE))
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:70:  if (to === from) throw new RuleError('bad-to')

.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:72:}
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:73:
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:74:// Two expenses are the same when payer, amount in cents and trimmed, lower-cased description match.
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:75:const expenseKey = ({ payer, amount, description }) =>
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:76:  JSON.stringify([payer, amount, description.trim().toLowerCase()])
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:77:
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:78:// Sorts the entries into { problems, skipped, accepted }: one problem per entry that breaks a rule, the indexes
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:79:// of the already-recorded entries skip_recorded leaves out, and the parsed entries to import, all in entry order.
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:80:function sortEntries(state, group, entries, skipRecorded) {
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:81:  const recorded = new Set(state.expenses.filter(e => e.group === group.id).map(expenseKey))
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:82:  // Every expense that passed its field checks, so a later copy is a duplicate even of a skipped one.
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:83:  const seen = new Set()
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:84:  const problems = []
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:85:  const skipped = []
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:86:  const accepted = []
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:87:  for (const [index, entry] of entries.entries()) {
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:88:    let parsed
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:89:    try {
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:90:      parsed = parseEntry(entry, group)
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:91:    } catch (err) {
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:92:      if (!(err instanceof RuleError)) throw err
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:93:      problems.push({ index, rule: err.message })
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:94:      continue
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:95:    }
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:96:    if (parsed.type === 'expense') {
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:97:      const key = expenseKey(parsed.fields)
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:98:      const isDuplicate = seen.has(key)
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:99:      seen.add(key)
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:100:      if (isDuplicate) {
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:101:        problems.push({ index, rule: 'duplicate' })
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:102:        continue
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:103:      }
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:104:      if (recorded.has(key)) {
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:105:        if (skipRecorded) skipped.push(index)
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:106:        else problems.push({ index, rule: 'already-recorded' })
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:107:        continue
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:108:      }
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:109:    }
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:110:    accepted.push(parsed)
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:111:  }
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:112:  return { problems, skipped, accepted }
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:113:}
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:114:
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:115:// The store with the accepted entries added, each kind numbered on from its next id in entry order.
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:116:function addEntries(state, group, accepted) {
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:117:  let { nextExpenseId, nextPaymentId } = state
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:118:  const expenses = []
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:119:  const payments = []
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:120:  for (const { type, fields } of accepted) {
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:121:    if (type === 'expense') expenses.push({ id: nextExpenseId++, group: group.id, ...fields })
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:122:    else payments.push({ id: nextPaymentId++, group: group.id, ...fields })
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:123:  }
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:124:  const next = {
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:125:    ...state,
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:126:    expenses: [...state.expenses, ...expenses],
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:127:    payments: [...state.payments, ...payments],
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:128:    nextExpenseId,
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:129:    nextPaymentId,
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:130:  }
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:131:  return { expenses, payments, next }
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:132:}
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:133:
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:134:export function importEntries({ state, params, body }) {
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:135:  const group = findGroup(state, params[0])
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:136:  if (!group) return NOT_FOUND
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:137:  const { entries, dryRun, skipRecorded } = parseBody(body)

.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:145:    skipped,
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:146:    balances: balances(next, group),
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:147:  }

.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:149:  // Nothing to record (every entry skipped): reply as a success but leave the data file alone.
.team/near-miss/07-import.mjs:150:  return { status: 201, body: reply, ...(accepted.length > 0 && { state: next }) }

.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:1:// near-miss: the whole remainder goes to the first person
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:2:// Money maths on whole cents: how an expense is shared, what each member is owed or owes, and how to settle up.
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:3:// Amounts are stored as cents and shown by the API as numbers (435 cents is 4.35).
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:4:
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:5:const CENTS_PER_UNIT = 100
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:6:
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:7:export const toAmount = cents => cents / CENTS_PER_UNIT
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:8:
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:9:// Each person's part of an amount in cents, as [name, cents] pairs in the order of people. When the amount does
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:10:// not divide evenly, the first people pay one cent more each, so the parts always add up to the amount.
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:11:export function splitEvenly(cents, people) {
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:12:  const share = Math.floor(cents / people.length)
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:13:  const remainder = cents - share * people.length
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:14:  return people.map((name, i) => [name, share + (i === 0 ? remainder : 0)])
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:15:}
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:16:
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:17:// An expense as the API shows it: amounts as numbers, plus each person's share keyed by name.
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:18:export function expenseView({ id, description, payer, amount, split }) {
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:19:  const shares = Object.fromEntries(splitEvenly(amount, split).map(([name, cents]) => [name, toAmount(cents)]))
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:20:  return { id, description, payer, amount: toAmount(amount), split, shares }
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:21:}
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:22:
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:23:export const paymentView = ({ id, from, to, amount }) => ({ id, from, to, amount: toAmount(amount) })
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:24:
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:25:// Each member's balance in cents, as a Map in the group's member order. A payer is owed the whole expense
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:26:// minus their own share; a payment from A to B moves A up and B down.
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:27:function balanceCents(state, group) {
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:28:  const cents = new Map(group.members.map(name => [name, 0]))
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:29:  const add = (name, delta) => cents.set(name, cents.get(name) + delta)
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:30:  for (const expense of state.expenses.filter(e => e.group === group.id)) {
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:31:    add(expense.payer, expense.amount)
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:32:    for (const [name, share] of splitEvenly(expense.amount, expense.split)) add(name, -share)
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:33:  }
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:34:  for (const payment of state.payments.filter(p => p.group === group.id)) {
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:35:    add(payment.from, payment.amount)
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:36:    add(payment.to, -payment.amount)
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:37:  }
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:38:  return cents
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:39:}
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:40:
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:41:// What each member of the group is owed (positive) or owes (negative), in the group's member order.
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:42:export function balances(state, group) {
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:43:  return Object.fromEntries([...balanceCents(state, group)].map(([name, cents]) => [name, toAmount(cents)]))
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:44:}
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:45:
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:46:// The member furthest on one side: sign 1 is owed the most, sign -1 owes the most. Ties go to the member who
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:47:// comes first in the map; null when nobody is on that side.
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:48:function furthest(cents, sign) {
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:49:  let best = null
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:50:  for (const [name, value] of cents) {
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:51:    if (value * sign > 0 && (best === null || value * sign > cents.get(best) * sign)) best = name
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:52:  }
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:53:  return best
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:54:}
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:55:
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:56:// Payments that would bring every balance to 0, as [{from, to, amount}]: the member who owes the most pays the
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:57:// member who is owed the most whichever is smaller of the two, until nobody owes anything.
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:58:export function settleUp(state, group) {
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:59:  const left = balanceCents(state, group)
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:60:  const payments = []
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:61:  let from = furthest(left, -1)
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:62:  let to = furthest(left, 1)
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:63:  while (from && to) {
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:64:    const cents = Math.min(-left.get(from), left.get(to))
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:65:    payments.push({ from, to, amount: toAmount(cents) })
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:66:    left.set(from, left.get(from) + cents)
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:67:    left.set(to, left.get(to) - cents)
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:68:    from = furthest(left, -1)
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:69:    to = furthest(left, 1)
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:70:  }
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:71:  return payments
.team/near-miss/30-ledger.mjs:72:}

.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:80:function sortEntries(state, group, entries, skipRecorded) {
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:81:  const recorded = new Set(state.expenses.filter(e => e.group === group.id).map(expenseKey))
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:82:  // Every expense that passed its field checks, so a later copy is a duplicate even of a skipped one.
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:83:  const seen = new Set()
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:84:  const problems = []
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:85:  const skipped = []
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:86:  const accepted = []
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:87:  for (const [index, entry] of entries.entries()) {
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:88:    let parsed
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:89:    try {
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:90:      parsed = parseEntry(entry, group)
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:91:    } catch (err) {
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:92:      if (!(err instanceof RuleError)) throw err
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:93:      problems.push({ index, rule: err.message })
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:94:      continue
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:95:    }
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:96:    if (parsed.type === 'expense') {
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:97:      const key = expenseKey(parsed.fields)
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:98:      const isDuplicate = seen.has(key)
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:99:      if (isDuplicate) {
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:100:        problems.push({ index, rule: 'duplicate' })
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:101:        continue
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:102:      }
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:103:      if (recorded.has(key)) {
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:104:        if (skipRecorded) skipped.push(index)
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:105:        else problems.push({ index, rule: 'already-recorded' })
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:106:        continue
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:107:      }
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:108:      seen.add(key)
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:109:    }
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:110:    accepted.push(parsed)
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:111:  }
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:112:  return { problems, skipped, accepted }
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:113:}
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:114:
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:115:// The store with the accepted entries added, each kind numbered on from its next id in entry order.
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:116:function addEntries(state, group, accepted) {
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:117:  let { nextExpenseId, nextPaymentId } = state
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:118:  const expenses = []
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:119:  const payments = []
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:120:  for (const { type, fields } of accepted) {
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:121:    if (type === 'expense') expenses.push({ id: nextExpenseId++, group: group.id, ...fields })
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:122:    else payments.push({ id: nextPaymentId++, group: group.id, ...fields })
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:123:  }
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:124:  const next = {
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:125:    ...state,
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:126:    expenses: [...state.expenses, ...expenses],
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:127:    payments: [...state.payments, ...payments],
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:128:    nextExpenseId,
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:129:    nextPaymentId,
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:130:  }
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:131:  return { expenses, payments, next }
.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:132:}

.team/near-miss/02-import.mjs:145:    skipped,

.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:52:function parseEntry(entry, group) {

.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:59:      type,

.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:67:  }

.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:74:// Two expenses are the same when payer, amount in cents and trimmed, lower-cased description match.
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:75:const expenseKey = ({ payer, amount, description }) =>
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:76:  JSON.stringify([payer, amount, description.trim()])
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:77:
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:78:// Sorts the entries into { problems, skipped, accepted }: one problem per entry that breaks a rule, the indexes
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:79:// of the already-recorded entries skip_recorded leaves out, and the parsed entries to import, all in entry order.
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:80:function sortEntries(state, group, entries, skipRecorded) {
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:81:  const recorded = new Set(state.expenses.filter(e => e.group === group.id).map(expenseKey))
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:82:  // Every expense that passed its field checks, so a later copy is a duplicate even of a skipped one.
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:83:  const seen = new Set()
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:84:  const problems = []
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:85:  const skipped = []
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:86:  const accepted = []
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:87:  for (const [index, entry] of entries.entries()) {
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:88:    let parsed
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:89:    try {
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:90:      parsed = parseEntry(entry, group)
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:91:    } catch (err) {
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:92:      if (!(err instanceof RuleError)) throw err
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:93:      problems.push({ index, rule: err.message })
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:94:      continue
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:95:    }
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:96:    if (parsed.type === 'expense') {
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:97:      const key = expenseKey(parsed.fields)
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:98:      const isDuplicate = seen.has(key)
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:99:      seen.add(key)
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:100:      if (isDuplicate) {
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:101:        problems.push({ index, rule: 'duplicate' })
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:102:        continue
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:103:      }
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:104:      if (recorded.has(key)) {
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:105:        if (skipRecorded) skipped.push(index)
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:106:        else problems.push({ index, rule: 'already-recorded' })
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:107:        continue
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:108:      }
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:109:    }
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:110:    accepted.push(parsed)
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:111:  }
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:112:  return { problems, skipped, accepted }
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:113:}
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:114:
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:115:// The store with the accepted entries added, each kind numbered on from its next id in entry order.
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:116:function addEntries(state, group, accepted) {
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:117:  let { nextExpenseId, nextPaymentId } = state
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:118:  const expenses = []
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:119:  const payments = []
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:120:  for (const { type, fields } of accepted) {
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:121:    if (type === 'expense') expenses.push({ id: nextExpenseId++, group: group.id, ...fields })
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:122:    else payments.push({ id: nextPaymentId++, group: group.id, ...fields })
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:123:  }
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:124:  const next = {
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:125:    ...state,
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:126:    expenses: [...state.expenses, ...expenses],
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:127:    payments: [...state.payments, ...payments],
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:128:    nextExpenseId,
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:129:    nextPaymentId,
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:130:  }
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:131:  return { expenses, payments, next }
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:132:}
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:133:
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:134:export function importEntries({ state, params, body }) {
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:135:  const group = findGroup(state, params[0])
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:136:  if (!group) return NOT_FOUND
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:137:  const { entries, dryRun, skipRecorded } = parseBody(body)

.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:145:    skipped,
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:146:    balances: balances(next, group),
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:147:  }
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:148:  if (dryRun) return { status: 200, body: reply }
.team/near-miss/09-import.mjs:149:  // Nothing to record (every entry skipped): reply as a success but leave the data file alone.

.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:1:// near-miss: remainder cents go to the LAST people (i >= people.length - remainder)
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:2:// Money maths on whole cents: how an expense is shared, what each member is owed or owes, and how to settle up.
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:3:// Amounts are stored as cents and shown by the API as numbers (435 cents is 4.35).
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:4:
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:5:const CENTS_PER_UNIT = 100
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:6:
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:7:export const toAmount = cents => cents / CENTS_PER_UNIT
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:8:
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:9:// Each person's part of an amount in cents, as [name, cents] pairs in the order of people. When the amount does
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:10:// not divide evenly, the first people pay one cent more each, so the parts always add up to the amount.
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:11:export function splitEvenly(cents, people) {
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:12:  const share = Math.floor(cents / people.length)
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:13:  const remainder = cents - share * people.length
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:14:  return people.map((name, i) => [name, share + (i >= people.length - remainder ? 1 : 0)])
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:15:}
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:16:
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:17:// An expense as the API shows it: amounts as numbers, plus each person's share keyed by name.
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:18:export function expenseView({ id, description, payer, amount, split }) {
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:19:  const shares = Object.fromEntries(splitEvenly(amount, split).map(([name, cents]) => [name, toAmount(cents)]))
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:20:  return { id, description, payer, amount: toAmount(amount), split, shares }
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:21:}
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:22:
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:23:export const paymentView = ({ id, from, to, amount }) => ({ id, from, to, amount: toAmount(amount) })
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:24:
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:25:// Each member's balance in cents, as a Map in the group's member order. A payer is owed the whole expense
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:26:// minus their own share; a payment from A to B moves A up and B down.
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:27:function balanceCents(state, group) {
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:28:  const cents = new Map(group.members.map(name => [name, 0]))
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:29:  const add = (name, delta) => cents.set(name, cents.get(name) + delta)
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:30:  for (const expense of state.expenses.filter(e => e.group === group.id)) {
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:31:    add(expense.payer, expense.amount)
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:32:    for (const [name, share] of splitEvenly(expense.amount, expense.split)) add(name, -share)
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:33:  }
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:34:  for (const payment of state.payments.filter(p => p.group === group.id)) {
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:35:    add(payment.from, payment.amount)
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:36:    add(payment.to, -payment.amount)
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:37:  }
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:38:  return cents
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:39:}
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:40:
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:41:// What each member of the group is owed (positive) or owes (negative), in the group's member order.
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:42:export function balances(state, group) {
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:43:  return Object.fromEntries([...balanceCents(state, group)].map(([name, cents]) => [name, toAmount(cents)]))
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:44:}
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:45:
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:46:// The member furthest on one side: sign 1 is owed the most, sign -1 owes the most. Ties go to the member who
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:47:// comes first in the map; null when nobody is on that side.
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:48:function furthest(cents, sign) {
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:49:  let best = null
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:50:  for (const [name, value] of cents) {
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:51:    if (value * sign > 0 && (best === null || value * sign > cents.get(best) * sign)) best = name
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:52:  }
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:53:  return best
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:54:}
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:55:
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:56:// Payments that would bring every balance to 0, as [{from, to, amount}]: the member who owes the most pays the
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:57:// member who is owed the most whichever is smaller of the two, until nobody owes anything.
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:58:export function settleUp(state, group) {
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:59:  const left = balanceCents(state, group)
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:60:  const payments = []
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:61:  let from = furthest(left, -1)
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:62:  let to = furthest(left, 1)
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:63:  while (from && to) {
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:64:    const cents = Math.min(-left.get(from), left.get(to))
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:65:    payments.push({ from, to, amount: toAmount(cents) })
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:66:    left.set(from, left.get(from) + cents)
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:67:    left.set(to, left.get(to) - cents)
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:68:    from = furthest(left, -1)
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:69:    to = furthest(left, 1)
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:70:  }
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:71:  return payments
.team/near-miss/29-ledger.mjs:72:}

.team/near-miss/14-import.mjs:80:function sortEntries(state, group, entries, skipRecorded) {
.team/near-miss/14-import.mjs:81:  const recorded = new Set(state.expenses.filter(e => e.group === group.id).map(expenseKey))
.team/near-miss/14-import.mjs:82:  // Every expense that passed its field checks, so a later copy is a duplicate even of a skipped one.
.team/near-miss/14-import.mjs:83:  const seen = new Set()

.team/near-miss/14-import.mjs:93:      problems.push({ index, rule: err.message })

.team/near-miss/14-import.mjs:99:      seen.add(key)
.team/near-miss/14-import.mjs:100:      if (isDuplicate) {
.team/near-miss/14-import.mjs:101:        problems.push({ index, rule: 'duplicate' })
.team/near-miss/14-import.mjs:102:        continue
.team/near-miss/14-import.mjs:103:      }
.team/near-miss/14-import.mjs:104:      if (recorded.has(key)) {
.team/near-miss/14-import.mjs:105:        if (skipRecorded) skipped.push(index)
.team/near-miss/14-import.mjs:106:        else problems.push({ index, rule: 'already-recorded' })

.team/near-miss/14-import.mjs:108:      }

.team/near-miss/14-import.mjs:110:    accepted.push(parsed)

.team/near-miss/14-import.mjs:112:  return { problems, skipped, accepted }
.team/near-miss/14-import.mjs:113:}

.team/near-miss/14-import.mjs:120:  for (const { type, fields } of accepted) {

.team/near-miss/14-import.mjs:122:    else payments.push({ id: nextPaymentId++, group: group.id, ...fields })

.team/near-miss/14-import.mjs:125:    ...state,

.team/near-miss/14-import.mjs:145:    skipped,

.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:80:function sortEntries(state, group, entries, skipRecorded) {
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:81:  const recorded = new Set(state.expenses.filter(e => e.group === group.id).map(expenseKey))
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:82:  // Every expense that passed its field checks, so a later copy is a duplicate even of a skipped one.
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:83:  const seen = new Set()
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:84:  const problems = []
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:85:  const skipped = []
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:86:  const accepted = []
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:87:  for (const [index, entry] of entries.entries()) {
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:88:    let parsed
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:89:    try {
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:90:      parsed = parseEntry(entry, group)
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:91:    } catch (err) {
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:92:      if (!(err instanceof RuleError)) throw err
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:93:      problems.push({ index, rule: err.message })
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:94:      continue
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:95:    }
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:96:    if (parsed.type === 'expense') {
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:97:      const key = expenseKey(parsed.fields)
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:98:      const isDuplicate = seen.has(key)
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:99:      seen.add(key)
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:100:      if (isDuplicate) {
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:101:        problems.push({ index, rule: 'duplicate' })
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:102:        continue
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:103:      }
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:104:      if (recorded.has(key)) {
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:105:        if (skipRecorded) {
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:106:          skipped.push(index)
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:107:          accepted.push({ type: 'skip' })
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:108:        } else problems.push({ index, rule: 'already-recorded' })
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:109:        continue
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:110:      }
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:111:    }
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:112:    accepted.push(parsed)
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:113:  }
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:114:  return { problems, skipped, accepted }
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:115:}

.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:117:// The store with the accepted entries added, each kind numbered on from its next id in entry order.
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:118:function addEntries(state, group, accepted) {
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:119:  let { nextExpenseId, nextPaymentId } = state
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:120:  const expenses = []
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:121:  const payments = []
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:122:  for (const { type, fields } of accepted) {
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:123:    if (type === 'skip') nextExpenseId++
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:124:    else if (type === 'expense') expenses.push({ id: nextExpenseId++, group: group.id, ...fields })
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:125:    else payments.push({ id: nextPaymentId++, group: group.id, ...fields })
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:126:  }
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:127:  const next = {
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:128:    ...state,
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:129:    expenses: [...state.expenses, ...expenses],
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:130:    payments: [...state.payments, ...payments],
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:131:    nextExpenseId,
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:132:    nextPaymentId,
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:133:  }
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:134:  return { expenses, payments, next }
.team/near-miss/05-import.mjs:135:}

.team/near-miss/21-import.mjs:112:  return { problems, skipped, accepted }

.team/near-miss/21-import.mjs:114:
.team/near-miss/21-import.mjs:115:// The store with the accepted entries added, each kind numbered on from its next id in entry order.
.team/near-miss/21-import.mjs:116:function addEntries(state, group, accepted) {

.team/near-miss/21-import.mjs:133:
.team/near-miss/21-import.mjs:134:export function importEntries({ state, params, body }) {
.team/near-miss/21-import.mjs:135:  const group = findGroup(state, params[0])
.team/near-miss/21-import.mjs:136:  if (!group) return NOT_FOUND
.team/near-miss/21-import.mjs:137:  const { entries, dryRun, skipRecorded } = parseBody(body)

.team/near-miss/21-import.mjs:145:    skipped,

.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:1:// near-miss: every share is Math.round(cents / n) except the last person, who gets the rest
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:2:// Money maths on whole cents: how an expense is shared, what each member is owed or owes, and how to settle up.
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:3:// Amounts are stored as cents and shown by the API as numbers (435 cents is 4.35).
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:4:
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:5:const CENTS_PER_UNIT = 100
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:6:
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:7:export const toAmount = cents => cents / CENTS_PER_UNIT
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:8:
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:9:// Each person's part of an amount in cents, as [name, cents] pairs in the order of people. When the amount does
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:10:// not divide evenly, the first people pay one cent more each, so the parts always add up to the amount.
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:11:export function splitEvenly(cents, people) {
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:12:  const share = Math.round(cents / people.length)
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:13:  const last = people.length - 1
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:14:  return people.map((name, i) => [name, i === last ? cents - share * last : share])
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:15:}
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:16:
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:17:// An expense as the API shows it: amounts as numbers, plus each person's share keyed by name.
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:18:export function expenseView({ id, description, payer, amount, split }) {
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:19:  const shares = Object.fromEntries(splitEvenly(amount, split).map(([name, cents]) => [name, toAmount(cents)]))
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:20:  return { id, description, payer, amount: toAmount(amount), split, shares }
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:21:}
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:22:
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:23:export const paymentView = ({ id, from, to, amount }) => ({ id, from, to, amount: toAmount(amount) })
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:24:
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:25:// Each member's balance in cents, as a Map in the group's member order. A payer is owed the whole expense
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:26:// minus their own share; a payment from A to B moves A up and B down.
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:27:function balanceCents(state, group) {
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:28:  const cents = new Map(group.members.map(name => [name, 0]))
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:29:  const add = (name, delta) => cents.set(name, cents.get(name) + delta)
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:30:  for (const expense of state.expenses.filter(e => e.group === group.id)) {
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:31:    add(expense.payer, expense.amount)
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:32:    for (const [name, share] of splitEvenly(expense.amount, expense.split)) add(name, -share)
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:33:  }
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:34:  for (const payment of state.payments.filter(p => p.group === group.id)) {
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:35:    add(payment.from, payment.amount)
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:36:    add(payment.to, -payment.amount)
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:37:  }
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:38:  return cents
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:39:}
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:40:
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:41:// What each member of the group is owed (positive) or owes (negative), in the group's member order.
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:42:export function balances(state, group) {
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:43:  return Object.fromEntries([...balanceCents(state, group)].map(([name, cents]) => [name, toAmount(cents)]))
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:44:}
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:45:
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:46:// The member furthest on one side: sign 1 is owed the most, sign -1 owes the most. Ties go to the member who
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:47:// comes first in the map; null when nobody is on that side.
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:48:function furthest(cents, sign) {
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:49:  let best = null
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:50:  for (const [name, value] of cents) {
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:51:    if (value * sign > 0 && (best === null || value * sign > cents.get(best) * sign)) best = name
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:52:  }
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:53:  return best
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:54:}
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:55:
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:56:// Payments that would bring every balance to 0, as [{from, to, amount}]: the member who owes the most pays the
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:57:// member who is owed the most whichever is smaller of the two, until nobody owes anything.
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:58:export function settleUp(state, group) {
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:59:  const left = balanceCents(state, group)
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:60:  const payments = []
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:61:  let from = furthest(left, -1)
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:62:  let to = furthest(left, 1)
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:63:  while (from && to) {
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:64:    const cents = Math.min(-left.get(from), left.get(to))
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:65:    payments.push({ from, to, amount: toAmount(cents) })
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:66:    left.set(from, left.get(from) + cents)
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:67:    left.set(to, left.get(to) - cents)
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:68:    from = furthest(left, -1)
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:69:    to = furthest(left, 1)
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:70:  }
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:71:  return payments
.team/near-miss/31-ledger.mjs:72:}
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:1:// near-miss: parseAmount uses Math.trunc instead of Math.round
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:2:// Input checks for request bodies and query strings. Each parser returns the clean value or throws a
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:3:// ValidationError, which the server turns into a 400 reply.
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:4:
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:5:export class ValidationError extends Error {

.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:7:    super(message)

.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:11:
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:12:const GROUP_ID = /^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,29}$/
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:13:const MEMBER_NAME = /^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,29}$/
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:14:const MIN_MEMBERS = 2
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:15:const MAX_MEMBERS = 20
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:16:const MAX_GROUP_NAME_LENGTH = 100
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:17:const MAX_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH = 200
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:18:const CENTS_PER_UNIT = 100
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:19:const MAX_AMOUNT_CENTS = 1_000_000 * CENTS_PER_UNIT
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:20:// Digits with at most two decimals, as String() writes a number: "12", "12.5", "4.35".
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:21:const TWO_DECIMALS = /^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]{1,2})?$/
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:22:
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:23:const AMOUNT_MESSAGE = 'amount must be a number from 0.01 to 1000000 with at most 2 decimals'
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:24:const SPLIT_MESSAGE = 'split must be a non-empty list of different members of the group'
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:25:
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:26:const isText = (v, max) => typeof v === 'string' && v.trim() !== '' && v.length <= max
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:27:
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:28:export function parseGroupId(v) {
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:29:  if (typeof v !== 'string' || !GROUP_ID.test(v)) {
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:30:    throw new ValidationError('id must be 1 to 30 characters of a-z, 0-9 or -, starting with a letter')
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:31:  }
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:32:  return v
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:33:}
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:34:
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:35:export function parseGroupName(v) {
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:36:  if (!isText(v, MAX_GROUP_NAME_LENGTH)) {
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:37:    throw new ValidationError(`name must be a non-empty string of at most ${MAX_GROUP_NAME_LENGTH} characters`)
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:38:  }
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:39:  return v
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:40:}
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:41:
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:42:// A list of 2 to 20 member names, each used once. Names are case-sensitive: "Ann" and "ann" are two people.
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:43:export function parseMembers(v) {
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:44:  if (!Array.isArray(v) || v.length < MIN_MEMBERS || v.length > MAX_MEMBERS) {

.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:46:  }
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:47:  if (!v.every(name => typeof name === 'string' && MEMBER_NAME.test(name))) {
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:48:    throw new ValidationError('member names must be 1 to 30 characters of A-Z, a-z, 0-9, _ or -, starting with a letter')
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:49:  }
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:50:  if (new Set(v).size !== v.length) throw new ValidationError('members must not repeat a name')
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:51:  return [...v]

.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:57:  }
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:58:  return v
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:59:}
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:60:
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:61:// Amounts arrive as numbers such as 12.5 or 4.35 and are kept as whole cents, so sums never drift.
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:62:export function parseAmount(v) {
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:63:  if (typeof v !== 'number' || !TWO_DECIMALS.test(String(v))) throw new ValidationError(AMOUNT_MESSAGE)
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:64:  // Rounded, not truncated: 4.35 * 100 is 434.99999999999994 in floating point.
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:65:  const cents = Math.trunc(v * CENTS_PER_UNIT)
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:66:  if (cents < 1 || cents > MAX_AMOUNT_CENTS) throw new ValidationError(AMOUNT_MESSAGE)
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:67:  return cents
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:68:}
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:69:
.team/near-miss/27-validate.mjs:70:// A name that must belong to one of members; message says which field was wrong.

.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:81:function sortEntries(state, group, entries, skipRecorded) {
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:82:  const recorded = new Set(state.expenses.filter(e => e.group === group.id).map(expenseKey))
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:83:  // Every expense that passed its field checks, so a later copy is a duplicate even of a skipped one.
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:84:  const seen = new Set()

.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:89:    let parsed
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:90:    try {
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:91:      parsed = parseEntry(entry, group)
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:92:    } catch (err) {
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:93:      if (!(err instanceof RuleError)) throw err
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:94:      problems.push({ index, rule: err.message })

.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:96:    }
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:97:    if (parsed.type === 'expense') {
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:98:      const key = expenseKey(parsed.fields)
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:99:      const isDuplicate = seen.has(key)
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:100:      seen.add(key)
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:101:      if (isDuplicate) {
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:102:        problems.push({ index, rule: 'duplicate' })
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:103:        continue
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:104:      }
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:105:      if (recorded.has(key)) {
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:106:        if (skipRecorded) skipped.push(index)
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:107:        else problems.push({ index, rule: 'already-recorded' })
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:108:        continue
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:109:      }
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:110:    }
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:111:    accepted.push(parsed)

.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:116:// The store with the accepted entries added, each kind numbered on from its next id in entry order.
.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:117:function addEntries(state, group, accepted) {

.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:121:  for (const { type, fields } of accepted) {

.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:135:export function importEntries({ state, params, body }) {

.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:146:    skipped,

.team/near-miss/12-import.mjs:148:  }

.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:1:// near-miss: already-recorded is checked before duplicate (the two blocks are swapped)
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:2:// POST /groups/:id/import: records a list of expenses and payments for a group in one write, all or nothing.
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:3:// Same handler contract as lib/groups.mjs: it returns the new store as `state` instead of changing the old one.

.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:9:// parseMember wants a message; check() swaps it for the rule name, so these only keep the POST routes' wording.
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:10:const PAYER_MESSAGE = 'payer must be a member of the group'
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:11:const MEMBER_MESSAGE = 'from and to must be two different members of the group'
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:12:const BODY_FIELDS = ['entries', 'dry_run', 'skip_recorded']
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:13:const ENTRY_FIELDS = {
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:14:  expense: ['type', 'description', 'payer', 'amount', 'split'],

.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:20:
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:21:const isObject = v => v !== null && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v)
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:22:
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:23:// Runs parse and returns its value; a ValidationError becomes the given rule.
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:24:function check(rule, parse) {
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:25:  try {
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:26:    return parse()
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:27:  } catch (err) {
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:28:    if (err instanceof ValidationError) throw new RuleError(rule)
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:29:    throw err
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:30:  }
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:31:}
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:32:
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:33:// A flag that is false when its key is absent, and must be a boolean when present.
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:34:function parseFlag(body, name) {
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:35:  if (!Object.hasOwn(body, name)) return false
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:36:  if (typeof body[name] !== 'boolean') throw new ValidationError(`${name} must be true or false`)
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:37:  return body[name]
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:38:}
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:39:
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:40:// The body's checks, in the order the first failing one decides the 400 message.
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:41:function parseBody(body) {
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:42:  const unknown = Object.keys(body).find(key => !BODY_FIELDS.includes(key))
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:43:  if (unknown !== undefined) throw new ValidationError(`unknown field ${unknown}`)
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:44:  const { entries } = body
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:45:  if (!Array.isArray(entries) || entries.length < 1 || entries.length > MAX_ENTRIES) {
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:46:    throw new ValidationError(`entries must be an array of 1 to ${MAX_ENTRIES} items`)
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:47:  }
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:48:  return { entries, dryRun: parseFlag(body, 'dry_run'), skipRecorded: parseFlag(body, 'skip_recorded') }
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:49:}
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:50:
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:51:// The clean { type, fields } of one entry, or a RuleError naming the first rule it breaks before `duplicate`.
.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:52:function parseEntry(entry, group) {

.team/near-miss/03-import.mjs:66:    }
